H..L\nyears=[data.cell(YEAR_ROW,c).value for c in YEAR_COLS]\nif any(y is None for y in years):\n    raise RuntimeError(f\"Could not read years from Data row {YEAR_ROW} H-L: {years}\")\n\n# Series codes in Data D21:D38, values in H-L\nDATA_MIN_ROW=21\nDATA_MAX_ROW=38\nCODE_COL=4  # D\n\nlookup={}  # (code, year) -> value\nfor r in range(DATA_MIN_ROW, DATA_MAX_ROW+1):\n    code=data.cell(r, CODE_COL).value\n    if not code:\n        continue\n    for year, c in zip(years, YEAR_COLS):\n        lookup[(code, year)] = data.cell(r, c).value\n\n# --- Step 1: write INDEX/MATCH formulas to 3 blocks ---\n# Use Data header row YEAR_ROW for year match\nformula_template=(\n    \"=INDEX(Data!$H$21:$L$38, \"\n    \"MATCH($D{row}, Data!$D$21:$D$38, 0), \"\n    \"MATCH({col}$10, Data!$H${year_row}:$L${year_row}, 0))\"\n)\n\nblocks=[(12,17),(19,24),(26,31)]\ncols=list(range(8,13)) # 35->12\n    use_row = base + 7       # 12->19\n    cap_row = base + 14      # 12->26\n    for c in cols:\n        col_letter=get_column_letter(c)\n        f=f\"=({col_letter}{base}-{col_letter}{use_row})/{col_letter}{cap_row}*100\"\n        task.cell(net_row,c).value = f\n\n# --- Step 2b: Summary statistics H42:L47 ---\nlabel_to_func={\n    'Minimum': 'MIN',\n    'Maximum': 'MAX',\n    'Median': 'MEDIAN',\n    'Simple mean': 'AVERAGE',\n    '25th percentile': 'PERCENTILE',\n    '75th percentile': 'PERCENTILE',\n}\n\nfor r in range(42,48):\n    label = task.cell(r,5).value  # blank\n    else:\n        # Excel stores numbers as text; keep reasonable precision\n        text = (\"{:.15g}\".format(float(value)))\n\n    v_el=cell.find('m:v',NS)\n    if v_el is None:\n        v_el=ET.SubElement(cell, f\"{{{NS['m']}}}v\")\n    v_el.text=text\n\n\n# --- Modify the Task sheet XML in the temp xlsx ---\nTASK_SHEET_XML_PATH=find_task_sheet_path(TMP, 'Task')\n\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(TMP,'r') as zin:\n    sheet_xml_bytes=zin.read(TASK_SHEET_XML_PATH)\n    root=ET.fromstring(sheet_xml_bytes)\n\n    #
